Actions

Languages None

CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The hymn leviathan can breathe air and water.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the hymn leviathan fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The hymn leviathan makes two Jaws attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Luring Presence.

Jaws. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 15 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage plus 14 (4d6) Thunder damage.

Trench Hymn (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature in a 60-foot Cone. Failure: 78 (12d12) Thunder damage, and the target has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: Half damage only.

Luring Presence. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature of the hymn leviathan's choice in a 120-foot Emanation originating from the hymn leviathan that can see or hear it. Failure: The target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn, and while Charmed this way, it must move toward the hymn leviathan by the safest available route on its turn and can't take Reactions.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the hymn leviathan can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Deep Surge. The hymn leviathan moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Jaws attack.

Tail Sweep. Strength Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature within 10 feet of the hymn leviathan. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: It announces itself with a subsonic trench-hymn that rolls through hulls and skulls alike, fighting the fleet before it ever breaches, then lunges to seize the largest vessel in its jaws and drag it down until the bones of the sea take it. Every round it sings again, layering crushing pressure over a lunging bite and a sweeping tail that founders anything still afloat, and under fire it never retreats — it descends into the cold dark to re-emerge where the fleet is thickest, patient and implacable as the trench itself.
[concept] lore: Born in the lightless pressure of the deepest trench, it has learned that the hulls crossing above ring sweetly when it sings, and it has spent ages learning exactly which notes make them fall.

```
Bog Hound
Medium monstrosity, neutral evil
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

22 (3d8 + 9)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +3, Perception +2

Saving Throws: 

STR +3

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

None

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

False Appearance. While the bog hound remains motionless, it is indistinguishable from an ordinary object.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the bog hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the bog hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the bog hound's turns. The bog hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the bog hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the bog hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the bog hound. If the bog hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.
Bog Slam (Bonus Action). One creature within 5 feet of the bog hound that hit it with an attack since the end of the bog hound's last turn takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage.

Actions
Multiattack: The bog hound makes two Bite attacks.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is also restrained.
```

Chapel Bell-Toller

Medium Undead, Neutral

AC 11
Initiative +1 (11)

HP 45 (10d8)

Speed 5 ft., Fly 40 ft. (hover)

MOD
SAVE

Resistances Acid, Bludgeoning, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Piercing, Slashing, Thunder

Immunities Necrotic, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Resonant Body. A creature that hits the chapel bell-toller with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 5 (1d10) Thunder damage and has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn.

Ethereal Sight. The chapel bell-toller can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The chapel bell-toller can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The chapel bell-toller makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Debt of Ruin.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Debt of Ruin. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target is cursed until the end of the chapel bell-toller's next turn. The first time the cursed target deals damage to any creature before then, it takes 7 (2d6) Necrotic damage.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the chapel bell-toller and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the chapel bell-toller's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the chapel bell-toller can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the chapel bell-toller; the chapel bell-toller disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The chapel bell-toller now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The chapel bell-toller can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The chapel bell-toller's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the chapel bell-toller leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the chapel bell-toller appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The chapel bell-toller casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The chapel bell-toller is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

[concept] identity: The bell-ringer's ghost opens a fight by drifting through the chapel wall and tolling its phantom bell, marking one living soul as 'the dying' so that all its strikes home on that target. Each round it hangs back behind stone and stalls, ringing again to drain warmth and breath until the marked victim fails, then it glides on to the next. It will not be cornered: if turned or bloodied it slips through a wall and waits, patient and unhurried, as if the death were already scheduled.
[concept] lore: A bell-ringer who rang for plague-dead until the plague took him, this ghost still keeps the death-roll and tolls only for those whose hour it has decided is come.

[concept] identity: The climbing owlbear opens a fight by dropping on prey from above, using its hooked foreclaws and owl-strong grip to seize a branch or ledge and swing down in a sudden, silent ambush. Every round after that it lunges in for a raking bite and claw strike, then scrambles back up the nearest trunk or rock face to reset the ambush, harrying the party from angles they can't reach. Under pressure it grows more frantic and daring, flinging itself from perch to perch and fighting to the death rather than abandoning its claimed cliff-face territory.
[concept] lore: A mountain-lineage owlbear whose generations in the crags have hardened its claws for gripping stone and bark, it claims a stretch of cliff or canopy and treats anything that climbs into it as prey that has already delivered itself.

```
Swarm of Drowned Choirs
Medium swarm of Tiny undead, neutral
Armor Class

14 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

180 (24d8 + 72)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

17 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

2 (-4)

Damage Resistances: 

bludgeoning, piercing, slashing

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, grappled, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned, prone, restrained, stunned

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 10

Languages: 

Common plus the languages it knew in life

Challenge

10 (5,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Aura of Truth. Any creature that starts its turn within 15 feet of the swarm must succeed on a DC 15 Charisma saving throw or, until the start of its next turn, be unable to speak a deliberate lie.
Swarm. The swarm can occupy another creature's space and vice versa, and the swarm can move through any opening large enough for a Tiny creature. The swarm can't regain hit points or gain temporary hit points.

Actions
Throats: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 0 ft., one target in the swarm's space. Hit: 61 (13d8 + 3) necrotic damage, or 30 (6d8 + 3) necrotic damage if the swarm has half of its hit points or fewer.
Luring Presence (Recharge 5-6): Each creature of the swarm's choice within 60 feet of the swarm that can see or hear it must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be charmed until the end of its next turn. While charmed this way, the target must move toward the swarm by the safest available route on its turn and can't take reactions.
```

**Concept:** name: Vampire | who keeps a garden of thorns

- clean
- read: def 10 / off 12 → 11; sustained 75 (2 × Grave Strike + legendary Deathless Strike); nova none; dmg 75

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, INT +8

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Sure Footing. Difficult terrain composed of its garden of thorns doesn't cost the vampire extra movement, and the vampire can move across its garden of thorns without needing to make an ability check.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day, or 4/Day in Lair). If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (Speed 5 ft., fly speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and Speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite. It can replace one attack with a use of Lingering Brand.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Lingering Brand: One creature within 60 feet of the vampire must succeed on a DC 17 Constitution saving throw or be branded: it takes 7 (2d6) necrotic damage at the start of each of its turns until it uses its action to pull the barbs free.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet of the vampire that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a vampire spawn under the vampire's control.
Legendary Actions

The vampire can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The vampire regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathless Strike: The vampire moves up to half its Speed, and it makes one Grave Strike attack.
Beguile: The vampire casts command, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17). The vampire can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

Pit Scree-Maw
Large elemental, unaligned
Armor Class

13 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

93 (11d10 + 33)

Speed: 

30 ft., burrow 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

poison

exhaustion, poisoned

tremorsense 60 ft., passive Perception 13

None

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Smother. As a bonus action, the scree-maw smothers one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While smothered, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the scree-maw, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the scree-maw's turns. The scree-maw can have only one creature smothered at a time. If the scree-maw takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the smothered creature, the scree-maw must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the scree-maw. If the scree-maw dies, a smothered creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.
Grinding Distraction (Bonus Action). One creature within 30 feet must succeed on a DC 14 Wisdom saving throw or, until the start of the scree-maw's next turn, have disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than the scree-maw.

Actions
Multiattack: The scree-maw makes two Talus Grip attacks.
Talus Grip: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
```

[concept] identity: The scree-maw lurks at the bottom of flooded quarry pits, indistinguishable from the rubble until it surges up in a churning landslide of loose stone to seize prey in its crushing talus-grip. Once it has a hold, it simply collapses backward and drags its catch down into the pit, smothering and burying it under an avalanche of living rubble — grinding and entombing every round until nothing moves. It is patient, mindless, and utterly relentless: a collapsed pit-thing that fights to the death because it cannot conceive of retreat.
[concept] lore: Born where a quarry's discarded spoil-heaps grew saturated with elemental earth, the pit-thing sinks anything that walks too close to its buried den, hoarding the drowned dead like buried treasure.

```
Glacier Heart
Huge elemental, neutral
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

210 (20d12 + 80)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

19 (+4)

	

6 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +9, Perception +5

Saving Throws: 

STR +9, CON +9

Damage Immunities: 

cold, poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

tremorsense 60 ft., passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Primordial

Challenge

15 (13,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Rime Aura. At the end of each of the glacier heart's turns, each creature within 5 feet of the glacier heart takes 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Cold Absorption. Whenever the glacier heart is subjected to cold damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the cold damage dealt.
Siege Monster. The glacier heart deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The glacier heart makes two Ice Leg attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Sundering Shove.
Ice Leg: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 22 (4d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Killing Cold (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.
Sundering Shove: Each creature in a 30-foot cone must succeed on a DC 17 Strength saving throw or be pushed up to 15 feet away from the glacier heart and be knocked prone.
```

Brass Tyrant

Huge Construct, Neutral Evil

AC 19
Initiative -1 (9)

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 16

Languages Common

CR 17 (XP 18,000; PB +6)

Traits

Searing Aura. At the end of each of the tyrant's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the tyrant takes 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Immutable Form. The tyrant can't shape-shift.

Siege Monster. The tyrant deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Brass Fist attacks.

Brass Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 20 (4d6 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Fire damage.

Furnace Breath (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Bonus Actions

Molten Retort. One creature within 5 feet that hit the tyrant with an attack roll since the end of the tyrant's last turn takes 14 (4d6) Fire damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Crushing Advance. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Brass Fist attack.

Siege Stride. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, it can move through Large or smaller objects and structures, destroying them.

Tremor Slam. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature within 10 feet of the tyrant. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

```
Reliquary-Glut
Huge ooze, unaligned
Armor Class

10 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

161 (17d12 + 51)

Speed: 

20 ft., climb 20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

6 (-2)

	

16 (+3)

	

2 (-4)

	

8 (-1)

	

1 (-5)

Damage Immunities: 

acid, necrotic

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, deafened, exhaustion, frightened, grappled, prone, restrained

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft. (blind beyond this radius), passive Perception 9

Languages: 

None

Challenge

9 (5,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Adhesive. The reliquary-glut adheres to anything that touches it. A Huge or smaller creature adhered to the reliquary-glut is also grappled by it (escape DC 15). Ability checks made to escape this grapple have disadvantage.
Amorphous. The reliquary-glut can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing.
Corrosive Form. A creature that touches the reliquary-glut or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 9 (2d8) necrotic damage.
Spider Climb. The reliquary-glut can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: The reliquary-glut makes two Pseudopod attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Hooked Lash.
Pseudopod: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 28 (7d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.
Hooked Lash: One creature within 30 feet of the reliquary-glut must succeed on a DC 15 Strength saving throw or be pulled up to 25 feet straight toward the reliquary-glut by a rippling pseudopod of gilt and bone and be grappled (escape DC 15).
```

Krennik Skullstandard
Small humanoid, chaotic evil
Armor Class

14 (leather)

Hit Points: 

77 (14d6 + 28)

Speed: 

50 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

	

14 (+2)

	

8 (-1)

	

12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

Perception +3, Stealth +5

Saving Throws: 

DEX +5

passive Perception 13

Languages: 

Common, Goblin

Challenge

4 (1,100 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Fear Aura. Krennik emanates an aura in a 20-foot-radius sphere while it is not incapacitated. Any enemy that starts its turn in the aura must succeed on a DC 13 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened until the start of its next turn.
Skirmisher. Krennik ignores difficult terrain, and opportunity attacks against it are made with disadvantage.
Mounted Agility. As a bonus action, Krennik takes the Dash or Disengage action.
Rampage (1/Day). As a bonus action, immediately after dealing damage to a creature that has half of its hit points or fewer, Krennik moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Banner Pole attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Krennik makes two attacks: one with Banner Pole and one with Hurl Javelin.
Banner Pole: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If Krennik moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 4 (1d8) piercing damage and, if it is Medium or smaller, is knocked prone.
Hurl Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 7 (1d8 + 3) piercing damage.
Wheeling Charge (Recharge 5-6): Krennik moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters must make a DC 13 Dexterity saving throw, taking 24 (7d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save and being knocked prone.

Languages None

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Spider Climb. The trophykeeper can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions

Multiattack. The trophykeeper makes two attacks, using Claw, Fang, or Claws in any combination.

Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Fang. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Piercing damage.

Claws. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 13 (2d8 + 4) Psychic damage.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The trophykeeper swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the trophykeeper, and takes 6 (1d12) Psychic damage at the start of each of the trophykeeper's turns. The trophykeeper can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the trophykeeper takes 12 damage or more in a single turn from the swallowed creature, the trophykeeper must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the trophykeeper. If the trophykeeper dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Driving Bellow (Recharge 5-6). Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature of the trophykeeper's choice in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the trophykeeper. Failure: The target immediately moves up to half its Speed in a direction of the trophykeeper's choice, using the safest available route.

[concept] synthesis text: A gaunt, soot-grey thing of matted astral fur and too many jointed, claw-tipped limbs, the Trophy Climber moves like a bankrupt noble picking through a ruin, and it kills not with fang but with the raw, flaying grief of the Astral Sea that soaks through its pelt. It opens by dropping from a ceiling onto the largest target, pinning them while its claws press that psychic dread straight through their skull and out their eyes; every round after, it drags its stunned prize toward its hoard, striking at anyone who follows and shrieking the concentrated terror of every soul it has ever collected. Bloodied, it does not flee — it climbs the walls and circles, herding survivors away from its lair with pulses of the same awful memory, fighting to the death to protect the collection it clearly believes it owns. It is fully sapient (INT 10-14), and it is a hoarder that drags its kills back to a lair of trophies.

Languages Primordial (Auran)

CR 5 (XP 1,800; PB +3)

Traits

Mimicry. The elemental can mimic animal sounds and humanoid voices. A creature that hears the sounds can tell they are imitations only with a successful DC 13 Wisdom (Insight) check.

Air Form. The elemental can enter a creature's space and stop there. It can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch without expending extra movement to do so.

Actions

Multiattack. The elemental makes two Thunderous Slam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Dazzling Burst.

Thunderous Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Thunder damage.

Dazzling Burst. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Blinded condition until the end of its next turn.

Whirlwind (Recharge 4-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature in the elemental's space. Failure: 24 (4d10 + 2) Thunder damage, and the target is pushed up to 20 feet straight away from the elemental and has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Every round it snatches another fragment of an opponent's voice — a grunt, a curse, a battle-cry — to thicken its own cacophony and add weight to its blows. It fights to the death in defense of its hoard of voices, never fleeing, because to flee is to lose the sound of itself.
[concept] lore: Born in the howling throat of a storm that swallowed a village's last words, this elemental drifts the ruined places of the world, hoarding speech and screaming back the voices of everyone it has ever crushed.

Huge monstrosity, unaligned

15 (natural armor)

184 (16d12 + 80)

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA

+3

Wounded Burst. The first time the hydra has half of its hit points or fewer in a fight, it releases a burst. Each creature within 15 feet of the hydra must make a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, taking 24 (7d6) poison damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also poisoned until the end of its next turn.
Hold Breath. The hydra can hold its breath for 1 hour.
Multiple Heads. The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead.
Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

Actions
Multiattack: The hydra makes as many Bite attacks as it has heads. It can replace one attack with a use of Dazzling Burst.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (1d10 + 5) piercing damage.
Dazzling Burst: One creature within 60 feet must succeed on a DC 16 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The Hydra lurches forward on gnarled necks, raking with jagged teeth while every wound it takes spills a choking column of smoke rather than regrowing — its stumps clot fast but the severed necks vent scalding ash that blinds and poisons attackers. Each round it presses the melee, whipping its smoking husks across the field to smoke out hiding prey, and under pressure it does not flee: the worse it is cut, the more smoke pours from its wounds, trading its own healing for a smothering cloud it hides inside.
[concept] lore: Bred from a hydra bloodline cursed by a failed ritual of reknitting, this monstrosity no longer regrows heads — it regrows smoke, and it wants to bury its old lair, and the memory of what it lost, beneath a permanent ash-fall.

**Concept:** a siege-engine golem of lashed timber that shoulders through walls

Ramshackle Siege Golem

Large Construct, Neutral

AC 15
Initiative -1 (9)

Speed 30 ft.

Immunities Poison, Thunder; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

CR 8 (XP 3,900; PB +3)

Traits

Resonant Body. A creature that hits the siege golem with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 7 (2d6) Thunder damage and has the Deafened condition until the end of its next turn.

Thunder Absorption. Whenever the siege golem is subjected to Thunder damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of Hit Points equal to the Thunder damage dealt.

Immutable Form. The siege golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The siege golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The siege golem makes two Iron-Shod Beam attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Sundering Shove.

Iron-Shod Beam. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 14 (2d8 + 5) Bludgeoning damage plus 11 (2d10) Thunder damage.

Sundering Shove. Strength Saving Throw: DC 16, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: The target is pushed up to 15 feet away from the siege golem and has the Prone condition.

[concept] identity: A ramshackle battering-ram given legs, it trundles forward in a straight line and simply does not stop for walls, doors, or defenders — it lowers its lashed-timber shoulder and drives through, splintering masonry and scattering anyone caught in the breach. Every round after the first it pivots to the next obstruction, ignoring strikes that would fell a softer golem, because a body of bound beams and iron dogs flexes and gives rather than shattering. It never flees and never toys; it is a patient engine that has one motion, and that motion is forward until the structure — or the line — comes down.
[concept] lore: Built by a besieging army that ran out of siege towers, the last ram-golem was lashed together from felled trees, wagon axles, and the iron bands of broken doors, and it still walks the ruined road, trying to finish a siege that ended with no one left to surrender.

Languages Understands Common plus two other languages but can't speak

CR 10 (XP 5,900; PB +4)

Traits

Weapon Lodge. When a creature hits the golem with a nonmagical weapon, the weapon lodges in the golem's body. A lodged weapon can be pulled free by a creature within reach that succeeds on a DC 17 Strength (Athletics) check as an action; while lodged, the golem can use it: its Slam attacks deal an extra 17 (5d6) Force damage per lodged weapon (maximum two).

Immutable Form. The golem can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The golem has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The golem makes two attacks, using Slam or Force Bolt in any combination.

Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 15 (2d8 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 9 (2d8) Force damage.

Force Bolt. Ranged Attack Roll: +9, range 120 ft. 22 (4d10) Force damage.

Trample (Recharge 5-6). The golem moves up to its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks and can move through the spaces of Large or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters is targeted once by the following effect. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 17. Failure: 35 (10d6) Force damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage, and the target is pushed 5 feet straight away.

Bonus Actions

Slow (Recharge 5-6). The golem casts the Slow spell, requiring no spell components and using Constitution as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17).

[concept] identity: A statue of a saint mid-weep, the Stone Golem opens by walking straight through cover and presses inexorably toward the nearest living thing, grinding stone fists down in heavy two-handed blows while tears of dust and grit trail from its unchanging face. Each round it advances without hesitation, shoving through walls and furniture instead of around them, and any who strike it feel the strike rebound off bodies that were carved, not grown. It neither flees nor gloats; it fights to the last chip of its stone, grief carved so deeply it has become weight.
[concept] lore: A cathedral mason carved a saint to mourn a drowned congregation, and something answered the tears by pouring itself into the finished stone so that the grief could keep moving — it walks because sorrow would not stay kneeling.

Vesp Lord

Large Celestial, Neutral Evil

AC 18
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 190 (20d10 + 80)

Speed 30 ft., Fly 30 ft. (hover)

MOD

Resistances Radiant

Immunities Charmed

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 11

Languages Common

CR 13 (XP 10,000; PB +5)

Traits

Halo of Pestilence. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, any creature that starts its turn in a 20-foot Emanation originating from the vesp lord. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the vesp lord, the vesp lord and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the vesp lord doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The vesp lord makes three attacks, using Scepter or Claw in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Leaden Touch.

Scepter. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 29 (7d6 + 5) Poison damage.

Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 29 (7d6 + 5) Slashing damage.

Leaden Touch. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: The target's Speed is halved and it can't take Reactions until the end of the vesp lord's next turn.

Reactions

Sovereign of the Swarm. While a creature with the Swarm tag is within 60 feet of the vesp lord and can hear it, the vesp lord issues a command to one such swarm; the swarm uses its Reaction to move up to its Speed or make one attack against a target the vesp lord names.

[concept] identity: The Plaguefather opens a fight by loosing its fly-swarm vassals in a crawling tide while it hangs back on droning wings, then wades in with a stinging scepter-strike to finish what the swarm began. Each round it reasserts command — driving its swarm over one target, and healing its own wounds from the flies that return fat and blood-hot — and it anchors the field with a halo of pestilence that sickens anyone who tries to stand beside it. It does not flee; when bloodied it turns its own failing body into a burst of flies, scattering to re-form rather than admit defeat.
[concept] lore: A lesser seraph cast down into the corpse-pits of a ruined heaven, it now reigns as archon over the flies that fed on its celestial flesh — it wants worshippers and hosts in equal measure, all of them breathing.

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Bloodied Burst. The first time the worm is Bloodied in a fight, it releases a burst. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21, each creature in a 30-foot Emanation originating from the worm. Failure: 42 (12d6) Slashing damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Tunneler. The worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Swallow.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Swallow. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature within 10 feet that the worm hit with a melee attack this turn. Failure: 42 (12d6) Piercing damage, and the target is swallowed. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the worm, and takes 22 (5d8) Acid damage at the start of each of the worm's turns. If the worm takes 40 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, it must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 10 feet of the worm and has the Prone condition. If the worm dies, a swallowed creature is no longer Restrained and can escape from the corpse using 15 feet of movement, exiting with the Prone condition.

```
Adult Red Dragon
Huge dragon, chaotic evil
Armor Class

19 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

256 (19d12 + 133)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


27 (+8)

	

10 (+0)

	

25 (+7)

	

16 (+3)

	

13 (+1)

	

23 (+6)

Skills: 

Perception +13, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6, WIS +7

Damage Immunities: 

fire

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 23

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Guarded Stance. While the red dragon has half of its hit points or more, melee attack rolls against it are made with disadvantage.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Sight Weakness. The red dragon has disadvantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Threshold Execute: One creature the red dragon can see within 90 feet that has half of its hit points or fewer must succeed on a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw or, if it has 24 hit points or fewer, drop to 0 hit points; otherwise, it takes 24 (7d6) fire damage.
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.

Large Fiend, Lawful Evil

AC 21
Initiative +2 (12)

Senses darkvision 120 ft.; Passive Perception 20

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Searing Aura. At the end of each of the pit fiend's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the pit fiend takes 13 (3d8) Fire damage.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Fiery Mace. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (4d6 + 8) Force damage plus 21 (6d6) Fire damage.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

```
Ossuary Archlich
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

Languages: 

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Radiant Aura. A creature that touches the archlich or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 10 (3d6) radiant damage.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the archlich fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the archlich reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the archlich's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the archlich is hit by an attack roll, the archlich casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The archlich makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Dazzling Burst or (B) Spellcasting to cast Guiding Bolt.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be paralyzed until the start of the archlich's next turn.
Spellcasting: The archlich casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Dazzling Burst: One creature within 60 feet must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the start of its next turn.
Legendary Actions

The archlich can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The archlich regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The archlich teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the archlich must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Whether it fails or succeeds, the archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The archlich casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The Bone Sun opens by hurling searing lances of radiance from its throne of fused vertebrae, keeping foes at the far edge of the void it orbits. Every round it repositions its bone-sun to keep prey in its burning corona, spending spells to pin enemies in place before a final, contemptuous barrage. It is ancient and patient, toying with challengers rather than fleeing — it has outlived every army sent against it and treats the fight as a tedious formality.
[concept] lore: An archlich who bound its soul not to a phylactery but to a collapsing star of ossified dead, it drifts the void seeking fresh bones to feed the sun that keeps it deathless.

```
Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (Natural Armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


30 (+10)

	

14 (+2)

	

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

28 (+9)

Skills: 

Insight +10, Perception +17, Persuasion +16, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CON +16, WIS +10, CHA +16

Damage Immunities: 

Fire

Senses: 

Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 27

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

24

Proficiency Bonus:

+7

Amphibious. The dragon can breathe air and water.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon can use its Frightful Presence. It then makes three attacks: one with its bite and two with its claws.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (2d10 + 10) piercing damage.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d6 + 10) slashing damage.
Tail: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) bludgeoning damage.
Frightful Presence: Each creature of the dragon's choice that is within 120 feet of the dragon and aware of it must succeed on a DC 24 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. If a creature's saving throw is successful or the effect ends for it, the creature is immune to the dragon's Frightful Presence for the next 24 hours.
Breath Weapons (Recharge 5–6): The dragon uses one of the following breath weapons.
Fire Breath: The dragon exhales fire in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: The dragon exhales gas in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 24 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on Strength-based attack rolls, Strength checks, and Strength saving throws for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
Change Shape: The dragon magically polymorphs into a humanoid or beast that has a challenge rating no higher than its own, or back into its true form. It reverts to its true form if it dies. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying is absorbed or borne by the new form (the dragon's choice).

In a new form, the dragon retains its alignment, hit points, Hit Dice, ability to speak, proficiencies, Legendary Resistance, lair actions, and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, as well as this action. Its statistics and capabilities are otherwise replaced by those of the new form, except any class features or legendary actions of that form.
Legendary Actions

The ancient gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The ancient gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Detect: The dragon makes a Wisdom (Perception) check.
Tail Attack: The dragon makes a tail attack.
Wing Attack (Costs 2 Actions): The dragon beats its wings. Each creature within 15 feet of the dragon must succeed on a DC 25 Dexterity saving throw or take 17 (2d6 + 10) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone. The dragon can then fly up to half its flying speed.
```

[concept] identity: The Ancient Gold Dragon opens with a controlled, almost judicial approach — a single searing arc of molten-gold breath laid across the battlefield to test the worthy, then low passes and sweeping talon strikes to separate the brave from the merely present. Every round after it fights as a duelist and mentor at once, granting quarter where it is earned and answering insolence with a blast that scours stone, its massive body moving with deliberate grace as it flies. Under pressure its temper is the most dangerous thing about it: it does not flee, but its patience thins into magnificent, calculated fury, and it will burn a field clean to force the survivors to prove themselves.
[concept] lore: An Ancient Gold Dragon, one of the last burning-lamps of the high mountain ring, guards the hoard it inherited from a vanished age and roams its high passes for the sake of finding mortals worth remembering.

**Concept:** name: Vashka the Red | A hobgoblin warlord in lacquered plate who fights from a shield wall and never breaks ranks.

Vashka the Red

Medium Humanoid, Lawful Evil

AC 19
Initiative +1 (11)

HP 93 (17d8 + 17)

Speed 30 ft.

Skills Athletics +7, Perception +3

Senses Passive Perception 13

Languages Common, Goblin

CR 6 (XP 2,300; PB +3)

Traits

Pack Tactics. Vashka has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of Vashka's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. Vashka makes two attacks, using Longsword or Longbow in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Driving Shout.

Longsword. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 22 (4d8 + 4) Slashing damage.

Longbow. Ranged Attack Roll: +7, range 150/600 ft. 22 (4d8 + 4) Piercing damage.

Driving Shout. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature of Vashka's choice in a 30-foot Emanation originating from Vashka. Failure: The target immediately moves up to half its Speed in a direction of Vashka's choice, using the safest available route.

Reactions

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of Vashka misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: Vashka makes one Longsword attack against that creature.

[concept] identity: Vashka the Red locks his lacquered plate shield wall and opens by advancing in step, letting his front rank eat the first charge while he calls the cadence that keeps the line dressed. Every round he fights as one body with his shield-brothers, hammering the gap anyone opens and punishing any foe who tries to slip past the wall rather than through it. He does not break: bloodied, he tightens the formation, trades ground for a better corridor, and grinds the fight to a standstill rather than flee or lose his ranks.
[concept] lore: Vashka the Red rose from a hobgoblin legion's iron discipline, and now he hires his drilled shield wall to whichever warlord will pay in plate and plunder — his one want being a battle line that never breaks.

## Scythe-Shell Harvester — in the run

**Concept:** A Large insectile horror. It has four scythe-arms, a spitting maw, and a shell that sheds acid. It fights by pinning prey with two arms and carving with the others.

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader | class |

- **dropped clause:** "a shell that sheds acid"
- **dropped clause:** "carving with the others"

Scythe-Shell Harvester

Large Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 15
Initiative -1 (9)

Skills Athletics +8, Perception +5

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 15

Languages None

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Acid-Dripping Shell. At the end of each of the scythe-shell harvester's turns, each creature in a 5-foot Emanation originating from the scythe-shell harvester takes 7 (2d6) Acid damage.

Actions

Multiattack. The scythe-shell harvester makes two Spitting Maw attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Binding Lash.

Spitting Maw. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16).

Binding Lash. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Restrained condition until the end of the scythe-shell harvester's next turn.

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The scythe-shell harvester swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the scythe-shell harvester, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the scythe-shell harvester's turns. The scythe-shell harvester can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the scythe-shell harvester takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the swallowed creature, the scythe-shell harvester must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the scythe-shell harvester. If the scythe-shell harvester dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

## Black Iron Vigil — in the run

**Concept:** a sentinel of black iron, AC 20, Huge, speed 20 feet

- reader · fail · name-mechanic: Actions/Warded Charge: the name promises movement the text lacks
- read: def 10 / off 8 → 9; sustained 50 (2 × Iron Fist); nova none; dmg 50

```
Black Iron Vigil
Huge construct, lawful neutral
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

168 (16d12 + 64)

Speed: 

20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

18 (+4)

	

7 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

3 (-4)

Skills: 

Perception +4

Saving Throws: 

CON +8

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 14

Languages: 

Common

Challenge

11 (7,200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Relentless Pursuit. While the vigil is moving toward a creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, the vigil's speed can't be reduced and difficult terrain doesn't cost it extra movement.
Ward Bond. The vigil is magically bound to a ward of its choosing, and it can have only one ward at a time. While the vigil is within 60 feet of its ward, half of any damage the ward takes (round up) is transferred to the vigil.
Regeneration. The vigil regains 10 hit points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 hit point.
Iron Retribution. As a bonus action, the vigil targets one creature within 5 feet that hit it with an attack roll since the end of the vigil's last turn. The target takes 10 (3d6) force damage.
Interpose (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the vigil that it guards is hit by an attack roll, the attack hits the vigil instead, and the vigil has resistance to the attack's damage.

Actions
Multiattack: The vigil makes two Iron Fist attacks.
Iron Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.
Warded Charge (2/Day): The vigil targets itself or one creature it can see within 60 feet. The target gains 24 (7d6) temporary hit points, and it gains a +2 bonus to AC until the end of the vigil's next turn.
```

[concept] identity: The Iron Sentinel opens by interposing its Huge, riveted bulk between the intruder and its charge, absorbing the first blows on AC 20 plating before answering with slow, crushing iron-fist strikes. Every round after, it advances one ponderous 20-foot step and slams the nearest threat, deliberately steering itself between enemies and the thing it wards rather than pursuing fleeing prey. It never flees and never taunts; at half hit points it simply plants itself, commits to fighting to the death, and lets its black iron body take the punishment it was built to endure.
[concept] lore: Forge-bound by a dead artificer to guard a sealed reliquary, the Iron Sentinel still stands in the dark, waiting for a hand that will never come to relieve it.

Bogwitch Molra

Medium Humanoid, Neutral

AC 15

Skills Arcana +6, History +6, Perception +4

Senses Passive Perception 14

Languages Common and any three languages, Sylvan

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Actions

Multiattack. Molra makes three Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Creeping Dread or (B) Spellcasting to cast Ray of Sickness.

Arcane Burst. Melee or Ranged Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft. 16 (3d8 + 3) Force damage.

Spellcasting. Molra casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 14):

- At Will: Detect Magic, Light, Mage Armor, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation
- 2/Day Each: Fireball, Invisibility
- 1/Day Each: Cone of Cold, Fly

Creeping Dread. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of Molra's next turn. If the target was already Frightened by this ability, it also takes 9 (2d8) Psychic damage.

Trespasser's Bane. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: 19 (3d12) Necrotic damage, and the target has the Poisoned condition until the end of its next turn.

Bonus Actions

Misty Step (3/Day). Molra casts Misty Step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). Molra casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

[concept] identity: The marsh witch opens with a serpent-lash of woven curse-venom, stripping her trespassers of their speed and their senses before a blade ever clears its sheath. Each round after she feeds the mire itself into her workings, wringing venom and confusion from the standing water to bind another attacker in place, so the fight is lost by inches of stolen action rather than by wounds. She is patient and delighted, and bloodied she will not flee a fight in her own swamp; she sinks into the reeds and starts the hexing again.
[concept] lore: A marsh witch is a swamp-dweller who traded her years to the mire for the power to punish those who cross it, and she wants nothing more than for every trespasser to leave a little of themselves behind in the water.

Aura of Authority. While in a 10-foot Emanation originating from the vigil, the vigil and its allies have Advantage on attack rolls and saving throws, provided the vigil doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Shield Bash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 5 feet that the vigil can see. Failure: 9 (2d4 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Medium humanoid, neutral

Blurred Form. Attack rolls against Ilvareth have disadvantage. If Ilvareth is hit by an attack roll, this trait is disabled until the end of its next turn.
Magic Resistance. Ilvareth has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.
Misty Step (3/Day). As a bonus action, Ilvareth casts misty step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.
Phase Slip (2/Day). As a bonus action, Ilvareth teleports up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ilvareth is hit by an attack roll, Ilvareth casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Ilvareth makes four Arcane Burst attacks.
Spellcasting: Ilvareth casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17):

At will:: detect magic, detect thoughts, disguise self, invisibility, light, mage armor, mage hand, prestidigitation
2/day each:: fly, lightning bolt
1/day each:: cone of cold, mind blank, scrying, teleport
Arcane Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 150 ft., one target. Hit: 27 (4d10 + 5) force damage.

[concept] identity: Ilvareth Dusksong opens from a distance, peeling the light away so that arrows of hardened shadow lance out of every dark corner while he remains somewhere the eye cannot quite fix. Each round he steps through a fold of gloom — across the room, behind a pillar, out of reach — trading a spell for footing and never letting a foe close enough to test his fragile elven frame. When pressed and bloodied, he does not flee outright but withdraws in deliberate, mocking steps, drawing pursuers through shadowed ground he has already prepared.
[concept] lore: Ilvareth Dusksong was an archmage who chose to step sideways out of the world into the seam between shadows, and now he shepherds would-be apprentices there, offering forbidden shortcuts through the dark at prices their souls were not warned about.

audit finding below confidence gate: Blurred Form Blurred Form grants disadvantage on attacks, but Protective Magic triggers on being hit by an attack roll; the two entries interact — being hit while blurred is possible only if the attacker overcomes disadvantage, and the trait's disable is keyed to that hit, which is adjudicable, but the trait does not state whether the disable also applies if the hit is prevented by Shield.

Swarm of Drowned Funeral Bells

Medium Swarm of Tiny Constructs, Unaligned

AC 15
Initiative +5 (15)

HP 178 (21d8 + 84)

Speed 40 ft., Fly 30 ft. (hover)

Resistances Bludgeoning, Piercing, Slashing

Immunities Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained, Stunned

Senses blindsight 30 ft. (blind beyond this radius); Passive Perception 10

Languages Understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

CR 13 (XP 10,000; PB +5)

Traits

Blood Frenzy. The swarm has Advantage on attack rolls against any creature that doesn't have all its Hit Points.

Swarm. The swarm can occupy another creature's space and vice versa, and the swarm can move through any opening large enough for a Tiny creature. The swarm can't regain Hit Points or gain Temporary Hit Points.

Actions

Iron Rim. Melee Attack Roll: +10, reach 5 ft. 90 (19d8 + 5) Bludgeoning damage, or 45 (9d8 + 5) Bludgeoning damage if the swarm is Bloodied.

Death Knell Toll (1/Day). Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 17, one Bloodied creature the swarm can see within 90 feet. Failure: If the target has 67 Hit Points or fewer, it drops to 0 Hit Points. Otherwise, it takes 67 (15d8) Necrotic damage.

[concept] identity: A churning flock of tarnished funeral bells drifts in over the dying, tolling in rising waves that mark the last moments of a life — it clusters above a fallen creature and peals louder the closer it comes to death, driving its rimmed iron mouths down to bleed the final breath. It opens by hovering just out of reach and layering its ringing into a numbing, disorienting knell, then descends as one mass of clappers and rims to batter and drain. Under pressure it does not flee — it answers injury by swelling its toll to a deafening din, and it keeps ringing long after the body beneath it stops breathing.
[concept] lore: These bells were cast for a hundred funerals in a drowned cathedral town and never rung when the flood took it; now they fly in search of the dying they were denied, so that every death gets its final toll.

```
Heron-Sworn Lizardfolk
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15

Hit Points: 

94 (21d8)

Speed: 

30 ft., climb 30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Perception +5, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common plus one other language, Draconic

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Evasion. If the lizardfolk is subjected to an effect that allows it to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, the lizardfolk instead takes no damage if it succeeds on the save and only half damage if it fails. It can't use this trait if it is incapacitated.
Hold Breath. The lizardfolk can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost the lizardfolk extra movement, and the lizardfolk can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Patient Defense. As a bonus action, the lizardfolk takes the Dodge action.
Cunning Action. As a bonus action, the lizardfolk takes the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.

Actions
Multiattack: The lizardfolk makes two attacks: one with its Unarmed Strike and one with its Dart.
Unarmed Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 23 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. Once on each of the lizardfolk's turns when it hits with this attack, the target is subjected to the following effect. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the lizardfolk's next turn. If the target is a creature, it is subjected to the following effect. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be poisoned. While poisoned this way, the target can't regain hit points. The condition lasts until the target finishes a long rest or is cured by the lesser restoration spell or similar magic. On a successful save, the target is immune to this creature's contagion for 24 hours.
Dart: Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) piercing damage.
```

[concept] identity: A lizardfolk monk opens by stalking along walls and ceilings, circling until the pack has its angles, then darts in to land a single flurry of scaled-fist strikes before springing back out of reach. Every round it repeats that rhythm — close, unload a breath-held combo, retreat — using its climb speed and patient cold to frustrate pursuers. Under pressure it never panics: it keeps its footing, yields ground only as a ploy, and fights to the death once its clutch-mates are in the fight.

CR 5 (XP 1,800; PB +3)

Traits

Uncanny Sense. The well-drowned can pinpoint the location of moving creatures within 60 feet of itself.

Actions

Multiattack. The well-drowned makes two Grab attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Drowning Terror.

Grab. Melee Attack Roll: +6 (with Advantage if the target is Grappled by the well-drowned), reach 5 ft. 12 (2d8 + 3) Bludgeoning damage plus 7 (2d6) Necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 14).

Drowning Terror. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of the well-drowned's next turn. If the target was already Frightened by this ability, it also takes 18 (4d8) Necrotic damage.

Bonus Actions

False Form. The well-drowned shape-shifts to resemble an ordinary object or fixture of the place it waits in while retaining its game statistics, or it returns to its true form. While it is motionless in its false form, it is indistinguishable from that feature.

[concept] identity: The thing in the well is a drowned, grave-fed lurker that hangs in the black water just below the coping stones, wearing the shape of a pale hand or a lost face until a bucket line drops and a wrist comes within reach. It fights by snapping shut around the limb with its grave-cold grip and dragging the victim down under the water, then holds and presses until the struggle stops. Half-starved and hollow, it does not pursue past the rim of the well; if the fight turns against it, it sinks to the bottom and waits, patient as the water, for the next hand that reaches in.
[concept] lore: It is what the well gave back — a body drowned or thrown there long ago, salted with other peoples' grief and small offerings, that learned to hunger and stayed below to feed on whatever came to draw water.

```
Meshrin
Large humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (breastplate)

Hit Points: 

120 (14d10 + 42)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

15 (+2)

Skills: 

Perception +4, Stealth +5

Saving Throws: 

DEX +5, WIS +4

Senses: 

passive Perception 14

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Wounded Frenzy. While Meshrin has half of its hit points or fewer, it has advantage on attack rolls and saving throws.
Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin. It can replace one attack with a use of Horrid Display.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) piercing damage. Whether the attack hits or misses, immediately after the attack, a javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp, and the target's speed is reduced by 10 feet until the start of Meshrin's next turn.
Horrid Display: Each creature of Meshrin's choice within 30 feet of it that can see or hear it must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of Meshrin until the end of its next turn.
```

[concept] identity: A greatsword skeleton wades in blade-first, sweeping its oversized weapon in wide arcs to keep multiple foes at reach before stepping in to trade blow for blow. Round after round it simply presses forward, absorbing hits that would fell a living soldier and answering each with a heavier overhead chop. It never tires, never routs, and never negotiates — it fights until its bones are shattered, silent and implacable.
[concept] lore: Raised from the unburied bones of an executed champion by a necromancer's careless ritual, it still believes it is fighting for its life in an arena that no longer exists.

**Concept:** a sellsword who hurls a handaxe and closes with a shortsword

```
Rook Harrowgate
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (studded leather, shield)

Hit Points: 

112 (15d8 + 45)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

15 (+2)

Skills: 

Athletics +10, Performance +5

Saving Throws: 

DEX +5, WIS +4

Senses: 

passive Perception 11

Languages: 

Common

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of Rook misses it with a melee attack roll, Rook makes one Handaxe attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: Rook makes three Handaxe attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shortsword.
Handaxe: Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) slashing damage, and Rook has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, his handaxe returns to Rook's grasp, and Rook has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Horrid Display: Each creature of Rook's choice within 30 feet of Rook that can see or hear it must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened until the end of its next turn.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet that Rook can see must make a DC 15 Strength saving throw, taking 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage on a failed save. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is also knocked prone.
```

[concept] identity: A lean sellsword who opens every fight by hurling his handaxe from a dozen paces, using the flinch to cross the ground and put his shortsword into the gap the throw opened. After that he works close and dirty, feinting a second throw to buy a half-step before driving in with the blade, only breaking off to recover his axe. Bloodied, he remembers the price of the contract more than the fight and looks for a line of retreat rather than dying for a purse.
[concept] lore: A discharged company blade who sells his arm to whoever pays and keeps his handaxe sharp against the day he must buy his own way out of a bad contract.

passive Perception 15

Challenge

3 (700 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Surprise Attack. During the first round of each combat, Halric has advantage on attack rolls against any creature that hasn't taken a turn. If Halric hits a creature during that round, the target takes an extra 6 (1d12) bludgeoning damage.
Divine Aid (3/Day). As a bonus action, Halric casts bless, dispel magic, healing word, or lesser restoration, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Actions
Multiattack: Halric makes two attacks: one with Mace and one with Radiant Flame.
Spellcasting: Halric casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Wisdom as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 13): -

At will: light, thaumaturgy

1/day each: spirit guardians
Radiant Flame: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d10) radiant damage.
Mace: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 6 (1d6 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 5 (2d4) radiant damage.
